ומן השבועות


And from oaths.²⁹², 2:5, *Shulchan Aruch, Orach Chayyim,156:71.*

footnotes:
²⁹² As in, "May God damn me if..." which contemporary English has shortened to (God) damn! Makor ha-Yirah: TY, Shevuot



## Paragraph 170

###### Sefer HaYirah 170

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/170](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/170)


ומן הכעס. כי כל הכועס מבלבל דעתו ורוחו ולא יתיצב באהבת הבורא. וכן אמרו רבותינו. לא תרתח ולא תחטא. ובכמה מקומות דבר שלמה בחכמתו. והסר כעס מלבך (קהלת יא). כי כעס בחיק כסילים ינוח (שם ז). רגזן לא עלה בידו אלא רגזנותו. וגם משה רבינו היה אב לחכמים ולנביאים כל מקום שבא לכלל כעס בא לכלל טעות.


And from anger, for anyone that becomes angry mixes up his reason with his spirit, and will not place himself in the love of the Creator. And so our Rabbis said, "do not get hot and you will not sin" (BT, Berachot 29b). And in several places [King] Solomon said in his wisdom, "remove anger from your heart," (Eccl. 11:10), "for anger rests in the bosom of fools" (Eccl. 7:9). Nothing will come to the hand of a quarrelsome person but his irritability. And even with Moses our Teacher, who was the greatest of the sages and the prophets, whenever he was under the influence of anger he came under the influence of mistaken judgment.²⁹³

footnotes:
²⁹³ Makor ha-Yirah: "Our sages said..." BT Berachot 29b. "Nothing will come to the hand of a quarrelsome person..." Kiddushin 41a, and see ch. 2 halachot de'ot halacha 3. (rambam?) And what is written there. And so Moshe our teacher…., Pesachim 66b. And see also Sifré Num. 157, "because he was under the influence of anger he was under the influence of mistaken judgment." Translation following Jastrow.



## Paragraph 171

###### Sefer HaYirah 171

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/171](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/171)


ואפילו אם עשה לך אדם שלא כהוגן אל תקפיד ולמוד מהלל ענותן ז"ל.


Even if someone treats you inappropriately, do not lose your temper. But learn from Hillel the modest one,²⁹⁴ of blessed memory.²⁹⁵ 31a.

footnotes:
²⁹⁴ ולמד מהלל ענותן. BT, Shabbat 31a. The reference is to a famous aggadic story of the sage, when a man made a bet that he could cause the patient Hillel to lose his temper and went to see him on a Friday afternoon before Shabbat, asking pointless questions to try to cause him to lose his patience.
²⁹⁵ Vilna, Zilber ed., ז"ל. May = זכרונו לברכה, "may his memory be for a blessing." Moriah: זָכוּר לַטוֹב , "may he be remembered for good." Me'ah She'arim:  זִכְרוֹנוֹ לִבְרָכָה.Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Shabbat

ואך להטיל אימה בתוך ביתו. מצוה להראות עצמו כעסן ודעתו מיושבת ורוחו נחה. ואל יטיל אימה יתירה.


The same goes for fear inside his home: it is a mitzvah to show himself like one who can get angry but whose mind is settled.²⁹⁶, Rambam, ibid. But he should not develop additional fear, but only a moderate amount.²⁹⁷

He should not smash dishes²⁹⁸ in his anger. He should not destroy anything at all.²⁹⁹ Id. (105b).

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/174](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/174)


ויכוף את יצרו מכל אשר יתאוה אף משיחה בטילה. כי מה יתרון בדברים שלא לצורך אין זה כי אם הרבות פשע.


Let him bend his inclination from anything which he may enjoy, even from idle chatter, for what gain is there in needless words? This is nothing but increasing liability for negligence.³⁰⁰

To bend his inclination: Pesachim 113b and in הגההת of the Vilna Gaon. Even from idle chatter: Chagigah 5b, Yoma 77a. Rambam ch.2 halachot de'ot halacha 14. "This is nothing but increase of sin..." Prov. 10:19 "where there is much talking, there is no lack of transgressing." JPS. "In many words..." Avot d'Rabbi Natan end of ch. 22.

**Be** beloved to your brothers and to your fellows.³⁰¹

footnotes:

## Paragraph 176

###### Sefer HaYirah 176

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/176](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/176)


ואל תשכב בלילה וקטטה לך עם אדם. אך לך ותרצה אותו עד שיתפייס. ואף אם חטא לך בקש ממנו פיוס. ואל תאמר הן חטא לי. עליו לבא לפייסני. כוף את יצרך ולך אליו. למען לא יגביה לבבך. ותהיה מתועב על פני הבורא שנאמר (משלי טז) תועבת ה' כל גבה לב.


You shall not even lie down at night if you have a quarrel with a person. But go and appease him until you both reconcile. But even if he has sinned against you, seek reconciliation from him. Do not say, "see,³⁰² he has sinned against me, it is up to him to reconcile with me," bend your inclination and go to him, so that your heart shall not grow proud and you shall be abominable in the eyes of³⁰³ the Creator. As it is said, "Every proud heart is an abomination to the LORD," (Prov. 16:5).³⁰⁴ 28a. "But even if he has sinned against you…" Yoma 87a, see there.

footnotes:
³⁰² Zilber ed.: הוא חטא לי, "he has sinned against me,"; others, הן חטא לי, "see, (he) has sinned against me."
³⁰³ Vilna, על פני הבורא, "in the face of the Creator"; Zilber ed.: בעיני הבורא, "in the eyes of the Creator"; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: לִפְנֵי הַב, "before the Creator."
³⁰⁴ Translation mine.Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Megillah



## Paragraph 177

###### Sefer HaYirah 177

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/177](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/177)


ומאד הזהר שלא לשקר אפילו בדבר הבאי. כי חלול השם יש בדבר ואף לא להשתכר בשקר כי יצא שכרו בהפסדו. כי הוא מארבע כתות שאינן מקבלות פני שכינה. כת שקרנים. כת חנפים. כת לצים. כת מספרי לשון הרע. וכן ענשו של בדאי שאפילו אומר אמת אין שומעין לו. וכל המשנה בדבורו כאילו עובד ע"ז כתיב הכא (בראשית כז) והייתי בעיניו כמתעתע. וכתיב התם (ירמיה י) הבל המה מעשה תעתועים.


**Be especially**³⁰⁵ careful not to lie, even in a words of a rhetorical phrase, for there is desecration of the [divine] Name in speech. And also not to be rewarded in a lie, for the gain will be offset by the loss, since the four classes of those who will not receive the face of the Shekhinah: the class of liars, the class of hypocrites,³⁰⁶ the class of scoffers and the class of slanderers. And such is the punishment of the liar, that even if he speaks the truth, no one believes him (BT, Sanhedrin, 89b). And anyone who repeats his speech is as if he were an idol-worshipper (Ibid., 92a). As is written in this case: "I shall seem to him a deceiver," (Gen. 27:12).³⁰⁷ [Regarding] anyone who changes his words [is written]: "They are vanity, the work of delusion," (Jer. 10:15).

footnotes:
³⁰⁵ Boldface in Moriah, Me'ah She'arim.
³⁰⁶ Or, flatterers, faithless, arbitrary, fickle.
³⁰⁷ Translation following Koren. Context: Jacob is reluctant to fool Isaac into giving him the blessing.

"And it transgresses…" Avot 3:17 שחוק וקלות ראש כו' ומש"כ. "And everyone who speaks frivolously…" Avodah Zarah 18b.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/184](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/184)


והוי דן את כל אדם לכף זכות. למען יעבירו מן השמים על פשעיך וידונו אותך לכף זכות:


Judge everyone in the scale of merit, for the sake that in Heaven they shall overlook your sins and judge you in the scale of merit.³¹⁷ 127b. *Magen Avraham* (?) § 156.

footnotes:
³¹⁷ i.e., give everyone the benefit of the doubt, so that God will give you the benefit of the doubt. Makor ha-Yirah: M. Avot 1:6, BT Shabbat



## Paragraph 185

**If**³¹⁸ you are with people that you are obligated to honor such as your father and your mother honor them and be fearful of them.³¹⁹ § 240.

## Paragraph 187

###### Sefer HaYirah 187

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/187](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/187)


ולא תסתור את דבריהם. אם אמרו דבר אפילו ידעת כי אינו כן אל תאמר לא כן היה.


And you shall not contradict³²¹ their words, if they say something, even if you know that it is not³²² so. Do not say, "it was not so."³²³ 11.

footnotes:
³²¹ Following Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: וְלֹא תִסְתּוֹר אֶת דִבְרֵיהֶם (qal: impf) "do not contradict"; Vilna, Zilber ed.: ולא תסתיר (hiphil: impf) "do not hide"
³²² Vilna, Zilber ed.: כי אינו כן. Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: כִּי אֵינֶנּוּ כֵן.
³²³ Makor ha-Yirah: Ibid. and Ibid. 32b. And Shulchan Aruch ibid. se'if



## Paragraph 188

###### Sefer HaYirah 188

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/188](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/188)


ואם יש להם דבר עם אדם אל תכריע ביניהם.


And if they have a word with a person, do not compromise between them.³²⁴

footnotes:
³²⁴ Following Sifra introd.: שני כתובים המכחישים...ויכריע ביניהם. Because this has ויכריע ביניהם. I.e., while generally peacemaking is commendable and commanded, when your own parents or someone else that you are obligated to honor is one of the parties to the dispute, you may not mediate because you should be showing favor to your parents (or whoever it is).Makor ha-Yirah: BT Kiddushin 31b; Shulchan Aruch, Yoreh De’ah § 240. From the the language of our rabbi it appears that he agrees with the opinion of (R. Menahem ha-Meiri) which is brought in the Tur (ibid.) that even to say something like the words of the father is forbidden. And see in the Beit Yosef where it is also explained this way. Also according to Rashi. What the halakhic decisors and Rashi adopted for the matter it is a negative law just so: if there is a division in his own words, it is also forbidden to harmonize them, according to the meaning of our rabbi.



## Paragraph 189

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/189](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/189)

Do not interrupt them, for even one who interrupts his fellow is called a fool.³²⁵

## Paragraph 190

The rule of the matter: everything that might be attached to their honor should be attached³²⁶ and not to his.³²⁷

footnotes:
³²⁶ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כל מה שיוכל לתלות הכבוד בהם [יתלה] ולא בו. The pointed eds. omit the square brackets.

## Paragraph 191

###### Sefer HaYirah 191

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/191](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/191)


ומאד יש להזהר בכבודם. כי הוקש כבודם לכבוד המקום. והמכבדם כאלו כבד המקום. והמצערם כאלו צער עצמו.


One must be very careful with their honor, for their honor is compared to the honor of the One Who Is Everywhere, and one who honors them is as if he honors the One Who Is Everywhere, but one who causes them pain is as if he pains himself.³²⁸

footnotes:
³²⁸ Vilna, Zilber ed., כאילו צער עצמו "as if he troubles himself"; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: כְּאִלּוּ צִעֵר (עַצְמוֹ) [הַמָּקוֹם]. "as if he troubled (himself) [the One Who Is Everywhere]." Although it would seem that the "right" choice is the one in square brackets, i.e., he troubles God, the idea that one who troubles his parents really makes trouble for himself feels very wise and true. R. Yonah would say, “how could someone cause damage to the One?”



## Paragraph 192

###### Sefer HaYirah 192

And whatever he may do for them, let it be by way of honoring them. For one [person] may feed his father pheasant³³⁰ and drive him out of the world³³¹ but someone else may make his father grind in the mill and bring him into everlasting life in the world-to-come (BT, Kiddushin 31a).³³² 74.

## Paragraph 195

היה אביו עובר על דברי תורה. אל יאמר לו אבא עברת על דברי תורה. אלא אומר לו מקרא כתוב בתורה כך הוא. והוא יבין מעצמו ואל תביישו.


If his father transgressed the words of the Torah, he must not say to him, "Father, you have transgressed the words of the Torah," but rather he says to him, "the verse is written in the Torah like so." And he shall understand on his own and he shall not be disgraced.³³³

And honor your elder brother. And the brother of your father. And the brother of your mother. And the husband of your mother. And the wife of your father.³⁴⁵ For on all of them the sages of blessed memory³⁴⁶ expounded: (BT, Ketubot 103a) from the verse, "Honor your father and your mother," (Exod. 20:12).³⁴⁷

**Do not**³⁵³ withhold good from one who deserves it (Prov. 3:27).³⁵⁴ 77a. If a person asks you for a favor or for a mitzvah, do not refuse to do it. But if you have no knowledge about the matter, say to him "do according to your wisdom."

## Paragraph 208

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/208](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/208)


והיה רגיל בצדקה כפי כחך. ולכל הפחות שעבד עצמך לבורא לתת כופר נפשך חק קבוע בכל שבוע פרוטה או מחצה. כי אפילו עני המתפרנס מן הצדקה יש לו לעשות צדקה.


Be accustomed to giving as much *tzedakah* as you can at the very least³⁵⁵ discipline yourself to ransom your soul.³⁵⁶ for one’s own salvation. A fixed law: every week a *pruta* or a half.³⁵⁷ For even³⁵⁸ a poor person who is supported from *tzedakah* must give *tzedakah*.³⁵⁹

## Paragraph 211

###### Sefer HaYirah 211

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/211](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/211)


ואל תקפוץ ידך מהלוות לעני בשעת דחקו. כי גדול המלוה יותר מן העושה צדקה. וצדקתו עומדת לעד. כי איננו חסר בדבר. ויחשוב כי לכמה הוא מלוה בלא רבית.


And do not close your hand³⁶². from lending to³⁶³ the poor at their most urgent time. For greater is the one who lends³⁶⁴ than the one who gives *tzedakah*, and his righteousness endures forever. For he does not lose the principal, and he should consider for how many gentiles³⁶⁵ he lends without interest.³⁶⁶

footnotes:
³⁶² As opposed to פותח את ידך ומסביע לכל חי רצון from Ashrei
³⁶³ The verb לוה in the hifil can also mean to escort (the dead), but context here indicates it means to lend money at interest. Zilber ed.: מלהלוות; others מהלוות. Zilber ed. may be a misprint or typo.
³⁶⁴ Vilna: כי גדול המלוה[לעני]יותר מן העושה, "for greater is the one who lends [to the poor] than one who gives…" This halachah is reminiscent of the Chinese proverb, "Give a man a fish and he'll eat for a day; teach him how to fish and he'll eat forever."
³⁶⁵ Vilna, Zilber ed. גוים; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: נָכְרִים. See above.
³⁶⁶ Referring to lending in the ordinary course of business: advancing the cost in order to keep a customer. This is not necessarily referring to moneylending.Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Yevamot 63a. "For the one who lends is greater…" Shabbat 63a, Sukkah 49b; SA 249:6.And know that lending is a positive mitzvah greater than any in the 613 by any counting of the mitzvot. Even for a rich person when he does not have … it fulfills a positive mitzvah as explained in Sukkah 49 and SA ibid. But the poor person takes precedence. And see the words of our rabbi in peirush to avot…



## Paragraph 212

###### Sefer HaYirah 212

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/212](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/212)


ואם יקשה בעיניו להלוותו בלא משכון ילוונו במשכון. ויקבע לו זמן להלואתו. ובתוך ימי ההלואה לא יקראהו לעשות מלאכתו או כל דבר. אם לא יתן לו שכרו משלם. שנראה כרבית.


But if it should be difficult in his eyes to lend without collateral, let him lend with collateral and set for him a time to pay it back.³⁶⁷ But during the term of the loan he is not to call upon him to do his labor or any thing if he will not pay him his complete salary, since it would be seen as interest.³⁶⁸ 64b; SA YD 166.

footnotes:
³⁶⁷ Vilna: להלואתו; Zilber ed.: להלוואתו; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: לְהַלְוַאַתוֹ. Makor ha-Yirah: …that he is not obligated to lend to him for an unlimited time…
³⁶⁸ Makor ha-Yirah: "…he shall not call upon him…" BT, Bava Metzia



## Paragraph 213

###### Sefer HaYirah 213

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/213](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/213)


ובמעשרות אל תרפה ידיך מליטלה כראוי. כיצד מכל דבר שמשתכר. הן ללמד. הן לכתוב. הן לעשות מלאכה. או אף אם מצא מציאה או נתנו לו במתנה. או כל ענין (שיבא) [שיהיה] הן כסף הן [שוה] זהב מן הכל יפריש מעשר. כי דבר זה אין לו ערך. כי את אשר אסר הבורא בכל דבר לנסותו התיר במעשר.


**And in tithes**,³⁶⁹ do not let your hand be lax from removing what is appropriate. How? From everything that he profits, whether to teach,³⁷⁰ whether to write, whether to do labor, or even if he should find a finding, or if they gave it to him as a gift, or anything that may come to be³⁷¹ whether money, or money’s value,³⁷² from everything let him set aside the tithe, for this thing has no value, since everything which the Creator forbids to try him in any case, He permits in tithing.³⁷³ (BT, *Ta'anit* 9a).

footnotes:
³⁶⁹ Boldface in Moriah. This indicates that observant Jews of the day were tithing their income. another reason R. Yonah would have been concerned about falling levels of observance.
³⁷⁰ Zilber ed.: ללמוד (qal: to learn); others לְלַמֵּד (pi'el: to teach). Although one certainly profits by learning, it is easier to see how one would tithe one's profits from teaching than from learning.
³⁷¹ Vilna: [שיהיה] (שיבא) "(that will come) [that may be]." Zilber ed.: שיבא. Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: שֶׁיִּהְיֶה. Translation attempts to capture the flavor of both variants.
³⁷² Vilna: הן כסף הן [שוה] זהב, "whether silver, whether [equal of] gold." Zilber ed.: הן כסף הן זהב, "whether silver, whether gold." Moriah:  [כֶּסֶף] (הֵן כֶּסֶף הֵן שְׁוֵה (זָהָבֶ "whether silver whether like unto (gold) [silver]. Me'ah She'arim: הֵן כֶּסֶף הֵן שָׁוֶה זָהָב, "whether silver, whether equal to gold." Translation follows Zilber ed. for simplicity and euphony.
³⁷³ כי את אשר אסר הבורא בכל דבר לנסותו התּיר במעשר. I.e., one may not eat forbidden foods or benefit from forbidden things but one must still tithe from them. לנסותו would generally mean to test a person as Abraham was tested.



## Paragraph 214

###### Sefer HaYirah 214

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/214](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/214)


אל תגנוב דעת הבריות ואל תסרב בחברך יותר מדאי. ואל תאמר לו דבר זה עשיתי בשבילך ולא עשית בשבילו. אך אם יטעה וידמה כי בשבילו עשית אין צריך לומר לו לא עשיתי בעבורך כי הוא מטעה עצמו ואין זה גניבת דעת. כללו של דבר כל גניבת דעת אסור.


**Do not** deceive [God's] creations, even gentiles.³⁷⁴ Do not hustle³⁷⁵ your fellow too much.³⁷⁶ And do not say to him, "I have done this thing for you," if you did not do it for him. But if he should err and it should seem that for him you have done it, there is no need to tell him, "I did not do it for your sake," for he has erred on his own and this is not deception. The rule of the matter is: all forms³⁷⁷ of deception are forbidden.³⁷⁸

footnotes:
³⁷⁴ Vilna: ואפילו דעת כותי; Zilber ed. ואפ' דעת כותי; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: וַאֲפִילוּ דַעְתּוֹ שֶׁל גוֹי. See above regarding the distinction between גוי and כותי. Pointed eds. include the Rabbinic redundant possessive, where unpointed eds. used Biblical construct. Again, one might not expect to have to see such sentiments in supposedly tolerant and pluralistic medieval Spain.
³⁷⁵ תסרהב, to press, hurry. As the previous line is about trickery, this might also suggest to "hustle" somebody as in to be dishonest!
³⁷⁶ Vilna, Zilber ed., מדאי; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: מִדַּי .
³⁷⁷ כל צד גניבת דעת אסור, lit., every side of deception is forbidden.
³⁷⁸ Vilna, Zilber ed.: אסור; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: אסורה.



## Paragraph 215

###### Sefer HaYirah 215

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/215](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/215)


ואל תזכור שם שמים לבטלה. כי המזכיר שם שמים לבטלה ראוי לנדותו. ואף לא לצורך במקום מטונף.


**Do not** mention the name of Heaven in vain, for anyone who takes the name of Heaven in vain should be excommunicated.³⁷⁹ (BT, *Nedarim* 7b) And not even of necessity [should one mention the Divine Name] in a filthy place.³⁸⁰ 12(b?)
Makor ha-Yirah: BT, *Chullin* 94a.

footnotes:
³⁷⁹ ראוי לנדותו pi'el of נדה = banish, excommunicate. Such a person is appropriate for being banned or shunned.
³⁸⁰ Vilna: ואף (לא) לצורך [לא] במקום מטונף. Zilber ed.: ואף לא לצורך במקום מטונף. "And not even for need in a defiled place." Moriah, ואף שלא לצורך בְמָקוֹם מְטֻנָּף, "and not even of necessity in a defiled place." (Note that it is pointed as "in a place" and not בִּמְקוֹם, "instead of.") Me'ah She'arim: ואף שלא לצורך [ולא] בְמָקוֹם מְטֻנָּף, "and not even of necessity, nor in a defiled place."Makor ha-Yirah: And to the matter of a defiled place, see BT Shabbat

## Paragraph 219

###### Sefer HaYirah 219

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/219](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/219)


ואל ישמע (שום) [שם] טומאה על שפתיך כמו שמות הקדשים יעקרו ויהרסו. וכשתזכירם לצורך בפני הגויים אמור בשפה רפה ובכובד ראש.


And let no impure name³⁸⁶ be heard upon your lips like the names of the Sodomites,³⁸⁷ may they be uprooted and destroyed!³⁸⁸ 63b; SA YD § 147:1. But when you must mention them in front of the gentiles³⁸⁹ speak in an undertone and with solemnity.³⁹⁰ 21b

footnotes:
³⁸⁶ Vilna:  טומאה [שום] (שם) ואל ישמע "and let no impure (name) [no impure thing] be heard…" Zilber ed.: ואל ישמע שום טומאה. "and let no impure thing…" Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: ואל יִשָׁמַע שֵׁם טֻמְאָה, "and let no impure name be heard…"
³⁸⁷ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כגון שמות עכו"ם = עובדי כוכבים ומזלות "such as the names of worshippers of stars and constellations… (i.e., idol worshippers)." Moriah, Me'ah She'arim, שׁמוֹת הַקְּדֵשִׁים. "The names of the Sodomites…"From the root ק-ד-ש, to be consecrated, set apart. In Biblical Hebrew, a קָדֵשׁ is a consecrated person, i.e., a cultic prostitute. In Rabbinic Hebrew, it has come to be associated with the sinners of Sodom. It would seem that from קָדוֹשׁ, holy, to קָדֵשׁ, a cultic whore, is not such a far distance. R. Yonah is making reference to the dregs of society, outcasts such as whores who are not to be mentioned in polite conversation.
³⁸⁸ Makor ha-Yirah: BT Sanhedrin
³⁸⁹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: הכותי; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: הגוים. See above.
³⁹⁰ Translation following Jastrow, after BT, Mo'ed Katon

וכל דבר נבול וצד דבר ערוה לא ישמע על פיך. כגון להזכיר זונה. או כל דבר נבלה. כי שוחה עמוקה פי זרות (משלי כב). מעמיקים לו גיהנם והופכים לו גזר דין של שבעים שנה לרעה. ואף השומעים ושותקים ונהנים בשמיעה ואינם הולכים משם. נכשלים בדבר.


And any lascivious talk³⁹¹ or kind of sexually immoral speech shall not be heard upon your mouth, such as mentioning a prostitute or any obscene thing.³⁹² 32a. See above. For "the mouth of a forbidden woman is a deep pit," (Prov. 22:14).³⁹³ These deepen *gehinnom* for him and it is appropriate that he should be liable for³⁹⁴ a [divine] decree of seventy years for punishment³⁹⁵ (BT *Shabbat* 33a). And even those who hear and are silent but enjoy hearing and do not leave from there come to grief on the matter.³⁹⁶

³⁹¹ וכל דבר נבול וצד דבר ערוה לא ישמע על פיך. Following Jastrow, ניבול פה = lascivious talk.
³⁹² Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Shabbat
³⁹³ Translation following JPS. Lit., "…a strange woman…"
³⁹⁴ והופכים לו גזר דין.
³⁹⁵ לרעה. Vilna, Zilber ed. include the abbreviation רח"ל (=רואה חוב לעצמו?) ("he sees an obligation for himself,"?)

ואל תחשוד חבירך כי שמא כשר הוא בדבר. וחושד בכשרים לוקה בגופו.


**Do not**³⁹⁷ suspect your fellow, since he may be worthy³⁹⁸ in the thing, and one who suspects the worthy is smitten with disease.³⁹⁹

footnotes:
³⁹⁷ Boldface in Moriah, Me'ah She'arim.
³⁹⁸ Lit., "kosher."
³⁹⁹ Translation following BT Yoma 19b: החושד...לוקה בגופו. (Jastrow). It might also be, "one who suspects the innocent will be punished with lashes," but as this is not one who accuses the innocent, there would be no objective way to tell if a person harbored suspicions, so it seems more likely R. Yonah is talking about divine punishment for suspecting the innocent.

And do not bring yourself into suspicion, being clean from God⁴⁰⁰ and from Israel, lest people should come to grief by your hand, since [God's] creations suspected you.⁴⁰¹ 83(b?) ,פ"ג ה"בYebamot 24b. "Lest they come to grief…" BT *Shabbat* 149b. Anyone whose fellow is punished on his account… and for this reason our Rabbi (mesik?) pardon all who suspect you, and see above #59 (which has to do with not lengthening public prayers for fear of being suspected as a scoffer). Pardon all who suspect you.⁴⁰²

## Paragraph 226

###### Sefer HaYirah 226

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/226](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/226)


והזהר שלא ימצא טינוף על בגדיך או שומן או כל דבר מאוס. כי המתלכלך ואינו נזהר בנקיות משניא אוהביו של מקום לבריות. שנאמר (משלי ח) כל משנאי אהבו מות אל תקרי משנאי אלא משניאי. ויראי השם שאינם משמרים עצמם בנקיות אומרים רואיהם כמה לומדי תורה מאוסים נמצא שם שמים מתחלל על ידיהם.


And take care that filth shall not be found upon your clothes or fat,⁴⁰⁷ or any loathsome thing. For one who soils himself⁴⁰⁸ and is not careful with cleanliness causes the lovers of God, the [divine] creations to hate and is worthy of the death penalty.⁴⁰⁹ As is said, "All they who hate me love death," (Prov. 8:36). Do not read *m'sanai*, "they who hate," but rather *mas'ni'ai*, "they who cause hate."⁴¹⁰ And people who see God-fearers who do not keep themselves in cleanliness say,⁴¹¹ 114a, and see BT *Ta'anit* 28a. “How many learners of Torah are filthy!” and the result is -- the Name of Heaven is desecrated by them?⁴¹², in that R. Yonah seems to be turning the lash on his own. Religious people of all stripes can seem self-righteous and off-putting, but here R. Yonah admits that those who learn Torah can go around filthy and causing disgust, therefore defiling God's image (God's brand name, as it were). I think of the ugly stereotype of the foul-smelling charedi Jew.

⁴⁰⁹ Vilna: ( חיי"מ (=חייב מיתה Others חייב מיתה.
⁴¹⁰ This is an especially difficult post-rabbinic idiom to translate, first because the wordplay is lost in English, but also because the verb שׂנא does not occur in the hiphil binyan according to Jastrow, Holladay, and Brown-Driver-Briggs.
⁴¹¹ אומרים רואיהם. Hebrew unclear. Makor ha-Yirah: BT Shabbat
⁴¹² An unusual and fascinating halachah

לא תאמר שום נחוש כי לא נחש ביעקב ולא קסם בישראל. (במדבר כג). ובכמה מקומות הזהירה תורה (דברים יח) לא ימצא (מנחש) [וגו' ומנחש] (ויקרא יט) לא תנחשו. ואין לנו שום סימן אלא מה שאמרו חכמינו ז"ל. ואם יש לו לאדם לעשות מצוה כגון (להחזר) [לחזור על] מסכתא או להשיב בנו אל הספר לא יאמר אמתין עד ראש חודש או עד הפסח. כי כל יום וכל עת טוב להתחיל ולעשות במצות. ומי שאינו מקפיד לעולם לא יקפידו עמו. אך המקפידים השטן עומד על ימינם לשטנם. להחזיקם בטעותם.


You shall not say a word of soothsaying, for "Lo, there is no augury in Jacob, no divining in Israel," (Num. 23:23).⁴¹⁷ In how many places does our Torah⁴¹⁸ warn "there shall not be found among you…or a soothsayer," (Deut. 18:10).⁴¹⁹ "You shall not practice divination or soothsaying," (Lev. 19:26).⁴²⁰ And we are not to make any sign but only what our sages of blessed memory have said.⁴²¹ And if a person has to do a *mitzvah* such as to return to the tractate [of the Talmud] or to make his son return to the book, he should not say "I shall wait until the new moon⁴²² or until Pesach," for every day and at every time it is good to begin to do *mitzvot*. And, one who does not lose his temper: they should never lose their tempers with him. But the ones who lose their tempers: the Accuser⁴²³ stands upon their right and their left, strengthening them in their error.⁴²⁴ 66a. "But only what our sages have said…" BT *Chullin* 95b. "But if a person has to do a mitzvah…" see SA YD 179:72. "But the ones who lose their temper:…" BT *Pesachim* 110b

ואל תרים זרועך על שום אדם להכותו. כי הסוטר לועו של חבירו כאלו סוטר לועו של שכינה שנאמר (איוב לח) וזרוע רמה תשבר. וראוי הוא להקבר חי שנאמר (שם כב) ואיש זרוע לו הארץ. ואפילו בהרמת יד נקרא רשע שנאמר (שמות ב) ויאמר לרשע למה תכה רעך. על כן יש לו לאדם להזהר מאוד מלהכות. אם לא בנו הקטן או תלמידו שהוא מוכיחו.


And you shall not raise your arm against any man to strike him, for the one who slaps a Jew’s face is as if he slaps the face of the Shekhinah,⁴²⁷ 58b). [as is said, "it is a snare for a man to rashly declare, 'it is holy,'" (Prov. 20:25)⁴²⁸ and his hand be cut off.⁴²⁹] As is said, "the upraised arm is broken," (Job 38:15).⁴³⁰ And he is fitting to be buried alive, as is said, "the mighty man, who has the earth," (Job 22:8).⁴³¹ And even when he raises his hand he is called wicked, as in "he said to the wicked one, why do you smite your fellow?" (Exod. 2:13). Therefore a person must take much care to avoid striking, if it is not his minor child or his student who he is rebuking.⁴³²

footnotes:
⁴²⁷ Lit., who slap's a Jew's jaw is as if he slapped the jaw of the Shekhinah (Jastrow, BT Sanhedrin
⁴²⁸ Meaning of the proof-text is unclear, but it seems to be a play on לועו, "his jaw" and ילע, "declare."
⁴²⁹ It is not appropriate to rush to judgment as to what he believes is holy and to strike someone over it. One that does so should be severely punished.
⁴³⁰ Brackets in Moriah, Me'ah She'arim; Vilna, Zilber ed. omit the proof-text and following phrase.
⁴³¹ Translation following Koren. The proof-text puns on זרוע, meaning both "arm" and "mighty," in the original context, it speaks of a powerful man having dominion over the earth, but in the rabbinic context he has the earth in a more literal sense, being buried in it.
⁴³² Makor ha-Yirah: BT Sanhedrin 58b. "And it is fitting for him to be buried…" see, so explains our rabbi there in the gemara in what they say (במאי דאמרינן) there is no fixing (תקנה) for him but burial, however Rashi does not explain it so. "If not his child…" BT Makkot 8a. "minor" Mo'ed Katan 17a. "Or his student…" Makkot ibid., Bava Batra 21a, SA YD 245:10.

You shall not whiten the face of your fellow in public, for one who whitens his fellow's face, it is considered blood, spilled blood, (as) you know: the red color (of the face) disappears and the white takes its place⁴³³ 58b (translation and citation following Jastrow). Vilna, Zilber ed.: תדע דעזיל סומקא ואתי חיורא; Moriah: סֻמְקָא. Me'ah She'arim: סֻמָּקָא. and he has no share in the world-to-come. הוי זהיר בכבוד אשתך כדברי רבותינו אוהב אשתו כגופו ומכבדה יותר מגופו [וכו'] כי דייך שהיא מגדלת בניך ומצלת עצמך מן החטא. והוי זהיר באונאתה כי דמעתה מצויה לפיכך אונאתה מצויה וקרובה כי שערי דמעה לא ננעלו.


Take care with the honor due your wife, according to our sages, "he loves his wife as himself, and honors her more than himself," (BT *Yebamot* 62b) for it is enough for you that she raises your children and saves you from sin. And be careful not to hurt her feelings, for her tears are frequent. Therefore her pain is immanent, for the gates of tears are not locked (BT *Bava Metzia* 59a).

Keep your secret and all the more so with another’s secret. "Keep the gates of your mouth shut from her that lies in your bosom," (Micah 7:5).⁴⁶¹

**Enjoy**⁴⁶² the *mitzvot*⁴⁶³ to the most of your ability. And adorn the mitzvah up to one-third of the [value of the] mitzvah. For example, if he found⁴⁶⁴ an *etrog* for 12 prutas⁴⁶⁵ for 12 coins…" (number abbreviated 10+2); אתרוג בשתים-עשרה פרוטות " …*etrog* for 12 *prutas." (*number written out in full). and he found another one more beautiful than it, he should add up to 18 prutas⁴⁶⁶. to get the more beautiful one.⁴⁶⁷ 133b. And what is written, "adorn the mitzvah…" BT Bava Kamma 9b, SA OC § 656:1 (71?) (he goes on at some length, citing the Gaon of Vilna).

## Paragraph 248

A *mitzvah* that has no interpretation: interpret it. And let it not be a light thing in your eyes, neither the *mitzvah*, nor the transgression.⁴⁶⁸ 63a 'at the time they bring in the dispersed (? -- פזר)…' "Let it not be a light thing…" M. *Avot* 2:1, 4:2.

## Paragraph 249

###### Sefer HaYirah 249

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/249](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/249)


ואל תברח מעשות מצוה לא בשביל יראת הפסד ולא מפני פחד עונש הגוף. כי כדאי היא המצוה לשמרך מכל היזק. ואם ח"ו יארע לך היזק. נאמן הוא בעל מלאכתך שימלא חסרונך שחסרת בעבורו.


You shall not flee from doing a mitzvah neither for fear of monetary⁴⁶⁹ loss nor because of fear of physical pain.⁴⁷⁰ For the *mitzvah* is worth it⁴⁷¹ to protect you from all danger. And if, God forbid,⁴⁷² (initials) ח"ו, as in Vilna, Zilber ed. some damages should befall you, the master of your labor⁴⁷³ is faithful to repay the loss that you incurred on its behalf.

footnotes:
⁴⁶⁹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: לא בשביל יראת הפסד ממון ולא, "neither for fear of monetary loss nor…"; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: לֹא בִשְׁבִיל יִרְאַת הֶפְסֵד ולא, "neither for fear of loss nor…" Translation follows Vilna, Zilber ed. for clarity.
⁴⁷⁰ פחד עונש הגוף. lit., "fear of punishment of the body." This is not just respect but outright fear. Not literal punishment, but the discomfort and risk associated with it.
⁴⁷¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כי כדאי היא המצוה. "for the mitzvah is worth it..."; Moriah, Me'ah She'arim: כִּי כְדָאִית הִיא הַמִּצְוָה
⁴⁷² Moriah: חס ושלום; Me'ah She'arim: חס וחלילה. Both roughly mean "God forbid," and the discrepancy is probably due to guessing what the mss. meant when it gave rashei teivot
⁴⁷³ בעל מלאכתך, the master of your labor, i.e. God -- The One who commanded you to do the thing that resulted in loss will repay your loss.



## Paragraph 250

###### Sefer HaYirah 250

בצאתך לדרך צא בכי טוב. והזהר בתפלת הדרך וזו היא. יה"ר מלפניך ה' או"א שתוליכנו לשלום ותצעידנו לשלום וסמכנו (לשלום) ותצילנו מכף אויב ואורב בדרך ותנחנו אל מחוז חפצנו בשלום ותחזירנו לביתנו לשלום כי אתה שומע תפלה בא"י שומע תפלה. וצריך לאמרה אפילו אם אינו רוצה ללכת כ"א פרסה. ובתוך מהלך פרסה. וכן אם שכח לאמרה ונזכר לאחר שהלך פרסה אומר בלי חתימת ברכה.


**When you go on your**⁴⁷⁴ way, go without any harm being done.⁴⁷⁵ And take care with [reciting] the Wayfarer's Prayer, and this is it: "May it be your will⁴⁷⁶, our god and god of our fathers,⁴⁷⁷ that you lead us in safety and direct us in safety, and support us in peace.⁴⁷⁸ And that you save us from the hand of all foes and those lying in wait on the road. And may you bring us to rest to the safe harbor of our desire in peace, and may you return us to our homes in peace, for you are the One who hears prayer. Blessed are You, the eternal, who hears prayer."⁴⁷⁹ And he must say it even if he does not want to go, except⁴⁸⁰ if he is only going a *parasang*.⁴⁸¹ [And he must say it] within walking a *parasang*.⁴⁸² And the same if he forgot to say it and remembered after walking more than a *parasang*, [but] he says it without the seal of the blessing.⁴⁸³" clause.
Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Bava Kamma 60b. "And take care to say the Wayfarer's..." BT *Berachot* 29b. SA OC § 110:4.

⁴⁷⁸ Vilna: ותסמכנו [לשלום], "and support us [in peace]."

If gentiles confront you⁴⁸⁴ do not change your manner of speaking as if you were not a Jew.⁴⁸⁵ Even if they say to you, "you are a gentile," say to them "I am a Jew."

## Paragraph 252

ולא תעבור על המכסים. אל תעמוד במקום סכנה ותסמוך על נס. אך תירא תמיד פן יגרום החטא וזכור ליעקב אבינו ויירא.. ויצר לו.


And do not cross the toll-collectors⁴⁸⁶ and get arrested⁴⁸⁷and put your dignity and your life at risk.⁴⁸⁸ 13:2). Apparently tax farmers were feared above all other brigands. It is related to יראה as well. You shall not stand in a place of danger and rely upon a miracle, but always fear lest it cause you to sin. But remember our father Jacob, "Then Jacob was greatly afraid and distressed…" (Gen. 32:8). Do not annoy [even] a gentile child for the gentiles⁴⁸⁹ bear grudges.⁴⁹⁰

⁴⁸⁷ Vilna: ותתּפש ותשת. Zilber ed.: ותשת , Hebrew unclear. Moriah, Me'ah She'arim, וְתִתָּפֵשׂ וְתָשִׂים , "and get arrested and put..."
⁴⁸⁸ יראתך ונפשך על קרן הצבי. Lit, "your fear and your soul upon the horn of the deer." I.e., following Jastrow, put them where they cannot be reclaimed. (See M. Kethubot

## Paragraph 253

###### Sefer HaYirah 253

אל תהי צדיק הרבה. ראית אשה טובעת בנהר אל תאמר אנוס ולא אראנה. וכיוצא בזה. שמעת איש אומר להכות חברו אל תאמר לא אגלה לו ואהיה רכיל. לא תעמוד על דם רעך ואמור לו כפי יכולתך שלא בלשון רכילות. ואז את נפשך הצלת.


**Do not**⁴⁹¹ be too righteous.⁴⁹² If you see a naked woman⁴⁹³ drowning in a river, do not say, "I shall flee and I shall not look at her," or things like this.⁴⁹⁴ If you hear a man saying [he will] kill⁴⁹⁵ his fellow, do not say, "I shall not reveal him and be a talebearer." Do not stand by the blood of your neighbor,⁴⁹⁶ but say to the best of your ability that it is not the language of talebearing, and then your life shall be saved.

footnotes:
⁴⁹¹ Boldface in Moriah, Me'ah She'arim.
⁴⁹² אל תהי צדיק הרבה. Perhaps, overly righteous? Senderos de Vida: No seas justo mucho demasiado, “Do not be too much of a just one.” This appears to be one of those places R. Yonah recognizes people's limitations.
⁴⁹³ Vilna, Zilber ed.: אשה ערומה טובעת בנהר, "a naked woman drowning in a river,"; Moriah, Me'ah She'rim omit the word ערומה, "naked," perhaps not wanting to include the phrase “naked woman” in a religious text. What an amazing dictum from this paragon of stern morality! This is akin to R. Bachyah Ibn Pakuda, the eleventh-century Babylonian philosopher saying that this is more than just fulfilling the nuts and bolts, but also that there are greater duties afoot.
⁴⁹⁴ וכיוצא בזה
⁴⁹⁵ lit., “saying to strike…”
⁴⁹⁶ "Do not go as a talebearer among your people; neither shall you stand by the blood of your neighbor" (Lev. 19:16) I.e., you will avoid the punishment for talebearing because you were fulfilling the mitzvah not to stand by the blood of your neighbor.Makor ha-Yirah: BT, Sotah 21b, “Who is pious? One who שוטה…” “If you hear of a man…” in the gemara ibid but see Rambam ch.1 laws of the murderer 14.

אל תשבח אשה ביפיה בפני חבירך פן על ידך יחמדנה וכתיב. לא תחמוד קרי ביה לא תחמיד וכן קרי ביה לא תחמד. אל תקשט נפשך ותלך בין הנשים כדי שיתאוו לך. ואל תרבה שיחה עמהן אפילו עם אשתך.


Do not praise a woman for her beauty in front of your fellow, lest you should make him desire her; when the verse, “you shall not covet,” (Exod. 20:16)⁴⁹⁷ can be read “you shall not covet” and also be read ⁴⁹⁸ “you shall not cause coveting.”⁴⁹⁹ Do not flirt with the women⁵⁰⁰ in order to seize them with desire for you.⁵⁰¹ And do not increase conversation with them,⁵⁰² 1:5. Zilber ed.: ואל תרבה שיחה עמה, “and do not increase chatter with her...”; Others, עמהן, “with them.” even with your wife.

footnotes:
⁴⁹⁷ Exod. 20:14 in JPS and Koren. It cannot be an error because both pointed eds. have this cite.
⁴⁹⁸ קרי ביה...וכן קרי ביה One might expect אל תקרא...אלא here, so it might have something to do with קֶרִי, having an emission of semen, which might be the eventual result of praising a woman’s beauty to another man, and which Judaism, especially mystical Judaism, frowns on, but this rendering seems the most likely.
⁴⁹⁹ Vilna: .וכתיב לא תחמוד. קרי ביה לא תחמיד וכן קרי ביה לא תחמד (This gives the form of the verb, in the qal in the original Biblical commandment, in the hiphil and in the piel. Jastrow lists the piel as synonymous with the qal, and lists no meaning in the hiphil). Zilber ed.: וכתוב לא תחמוד קרי ביה לא תחמוד וכן קרי ביה לא תחמד. “And the verse ‘you shall not covet’ can be read ‘you shall not covet,’ and can also be read you shall not make covet.’” (giving the verb in the qal form twice and the piel form once). Translation generally follows Zilber ed. for ease of understanding. Moriah: וּכְתִיב (שמות כ טז) לֹא תַחְמוֹד, קְרִי בֵהּ לֹא תַחְמִיד, וְכֵן קְרִי בֵהּ לֹא תְחַמֵד. Puts קְרִי in the imperative: “read it:...”. Me’ah She’arim: identical to Moriah but points קְרֵי.
⁵⁰⁰ Vilna, Zilber ed.: אל תקשט נפשך ותלך בין הנשים. Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: אַל תְקַשֵׁט עַצְמְךָ
⁵⁰¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כדי שתתאוינה לך; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְאַוּוּ לָךְ

ולא יעיין בבגדי צבע שלהן ואפילו שטוחין על הכותל. כללו של דבר כל מה שיוכל להתרחק מהן ממשאן וממתנן ודבורן יתרחק. כי תמיד יצר הרע בוער כאש להחטיא האדם. לכן צריך האדם להיות כל שעה יועץ תחבולות לעשות מלחמה עמו.


Nor should he look at their dyed clothes, even those stretched out on the wall [to dry]. The rule of the matter: whatever a man⁵⁰⁵ can do to distance himself from [women] and their business transactions⁵⁰⁶ and their speech, he should do to distance himself. For the evil inclination is forever burning like a fire to cause man to sin.⁵⁰⁷ Therefore a man must always be a military advisor to make war with the evil inclination.⁵⁰⁸ 30b; *Yebamot* 63b

**Do not**⁵⁰⁹ praise anyone in front of his enemy, since it makes him incensed against him and in the praise cause him to speak of his shortcomings,⁵¹⁰ 16a.

אל תכנס לביתך פתאום וכ"ש לבית חבירך.


Do not enter your home suddenly, and all the more so⁵¹⁴ for your neighbor’s house.⁵¹⁵

footnotes:
⁵¹⁴ Vilna, Zilber ed.: וכ"ש (=וכול שכן); pointed eds. resolve the abbreviation.

## Paragraph 260

###### Sefer HaYirah 260

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/260](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/260)


אל תיעץ אדם לפי דרכך. אלא תן עצה טובה והוגנת לכל מי שיועץ אתך לכבוד הבורא.


Do not advise⁵¹⁶ a person according to your way, but rather give advice that is good and befitting to whomever seeks counsel with you,⁵¹⁷ for the honor of the Creator.

footnotes:
⁵¹⁶ Moriah: אַל תְּיַעֵץ; Me’ah She’arim: אַל תְּיָעֵץ.

## Paragraph 261

###### Sefer HaYirah 261

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/261](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/261)


כללו של דבר כל דבר המסור ללבך שאין אדם מבחין למה אתה מתכוין לטובה או לרעה נאמר בו ויראת מאלהיך המכיר מחשבתך.


The rule of the matter: anything that is entrusted to your heart⁵¹⁸ 32b, *Bava Metzia* 58b. that a person cannot distinguish for what you intend, [whether] for good or for evil, on this it is said: “You shall fear your God,” (Lev. 19:14) the One who knows your thoughts.

footnotes:
⁵¹⁸ Translation following Jastrow. BT Kiddushin



## Paragraph 262

###### Sefer HaYirah 262

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/262](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/262)


אל תקרא שום אדם בכנויו אפי' שלא בפניו. ואפי' לומר לאדם כדי שיוכל להכירו. אך דחק עצמך בענין אחר פב"פ עד שיכירהו.


**Do not**⁵¹⁹ call any man by his attribute⁵²⁰, even [if] he is not in front of you, or even⁵²¹ to say [it] to a[nother] person so that he may be able to recognize him. Rather push yourself after [what] that person [is really about] until he can recognize him.⁵²²

footnotes:
⁵¹⁹ Boldface in Moriah only, but it seems to be a logical place for a section break.
⁵²⁰ e.g., “the blond one,” “the lame one,” “the Black one”, etc...
⁵²¹ Zilber ed.: אפילו...אפילו; others: אפילו...ואפילו.
⁵²² Vilna, Zilber ed.: אך דחק עצמך בענין אחר פב"פ עד שיכירהו. Moriah: אחֵר פְּלוֹנִי בֶּן פְּלוֹנִי, “other, another.” Me’ah She’arim: אַחַר פְּלוֹנִי בֶּן פְּלוֹנִי. “after so-and-so” i.e., about this person. Translation following Me’ah She’arim. Makor ha-Yirah: BT Bava Metzia 58b.



## Paragraph 263

###### Sefer HaYirah 263

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/263](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/263)


אל תקרא לשום אדם בשום דבר ניוול ואפי' לקטן אסור לקרותו מאוס דרך שחוק כי כולם נקיים וקדושים.


Do not call any one⁵²³ by the name of any disgraceful thing; it is forbidden to call even a child by any loathsome or repulsive⁵²⁴ name in a joking manner⁵²⁵ for they are all clean and holy.

footnotes:
⁵²³ Vilna, Zilber ed., Moriah: אל תקרא משום אדם מישראל, “do not call any Jew...” Me’ah She’arim omits מישראל, making it more inclusive. Translation follows Me’ah She’arim.
⁵²⁴ Vilna: מאוס: repulsive; Zilber ed.: מיאוס, loathsome. Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: מָאוּס. Translation preserves both options.
⁵²⁵ Zilber ed. omits דרך שְׂחוק which is found in the other eds.



## Paragraph 264

###### Sefer HaYirah 264

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/264](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/264)


ואל תשבח שום גוי לא בחן ולא ביופיו ולא במעשיו. וכשתראה גוי עושה טובה או נאמנות או צדקה דאג. כי חסד לאמים חטאת לישראל. ואל תדינם לכף זכות אם לא בפניהם מפני דרכי שלום.


And do not praise any gentile⁵²⁶ neither by grace, nor by beauty, but rather⁵²⁷ by his deeds. And when you see a gentile doing good, or righteousness, or dealing faithfully -- worry! for grace for the nations of the world is sin for Israel. And do not give them the benefit of the doubt⁵²⁸ (except in front of them for the sake of peace).⁵²⁹ 20a...SA YD § 151:14. (etc.) “and when you see gentiles doing good…” BT Bava Batra 10b. “And do not judge them in the scale of merit…” (etc.) “except in front of them…” this is not a prohibition except d’Rabbanan.

footnotes:
⁵²⁶ Vilna: כותי; others גוי. See above.
⁵²⁷ Vilna: אלא במעשיו, “…but only by his deeds.” Zilber ed.: ולא במעשיו, “…nor by his deeds.” Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: [ולא] (אלא). (“but only...”) [“neither”] “by his deeds.”
⁵²⁸ Lit., “and do not judge them in the scale of merit.”
⁵²⁹ Makor ha-Yirah: BT Avodah Zarah



## Paragraph 265

###### Sefer HaYirah 265

ואל תתן דיו לסופרי אומות העולם הכותבים שמות הטומאה ופסיליהם כי אסור לסייע ידי עוברי עבירה.


Now: do not give ink to gentile scribes⁵³⁰ who write the Names of impurity and its nullities⁵³¹ for it is forbidden to support the hands of transgressors.⁵³²

footnotes:
⁵³⁰ Vilna: סופר כותי; Zilber ed.: אומות העולם “of the nations of the world,” Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: אֻמוֹת-הָעוֹלָם
⁵³¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: הכותבים שמות הטואמה ופסוליה “the ones who write the names of impurity and its nullities”; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: …שׂמוֹת הַטֻמְאָה וּפְסִילֵיהֶם, “...the names of impurity and their nullities. I.e., what seems to be divine, but the nullities associated with it are idolatry.
⁵³² עוברי עבירה, lit., “who transgress transgressions.”Zilber’s comment mentions Rashi on BT Avodah Zarah 55a



## Paragraph 266

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/266](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/266)


השמר מלצער בע"ח הן בהמה הן עוף. וכ"ש שלא לצער אדם שהוא עשוי בצלם המקום.


**Be on guard against**⁵³³ causing pain to animals,⁵³⁴ whether beast or foul, and all the more so⁵³⁵ not to cause pain to a person, since he is made in the image of the Omnipresent One.⁵³⁶

footnotes:
⁵³³ Boldface in Moriah, Me’ah She’arim.
⁵³⁴ Vilna, Zilber ed. abbreviate בע"ח; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to בעלי-חיים
⁵³⁵ likewise, Vilna, Zilber ed. abbreviate וכ"ש; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to וְכָל-שֶׁכֵן

## Paragraph 267

###### Sefer HaYirah 267

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/267](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/267)


אם אתה רוצה לשכור פועלים ומצאת עניים יהיו עניים בני ביתך. ואך אל תבזה אותם. אך דרך כבוד תצוה להם. ותשלם שכרם משלם. ואל תדחם בלך ושוב. כי הרבה לאוין יש בדבר. ואליו הוא נושא את נפשו.


If you want to hire workers and you have found poor ones, let them be as members of your own family. And you shall surely not disdain them; you shall only command them in a manner of respect; and pay them their wages in full, neither shall you press them in saying, “go and come back”⁵³⁷ for there are many negative [commandments]⁵³⁸ about the matter⁵³⁹ and to it He may take his life in payment.⁵⁴⁰ 1:5; BT Bava Metzia 60b. SA YD § 249, §251. “ואל תדחם בלך ושוב” Bava Metzia 110b

⁵³⁸ unpointed eds. give Rabbinic plural לאוין; pointed eds. give Biblical plural לאוים.

⁵⁴⁰ Lit., “he lifts up his soul.” Vilna: ואליו הוא נושא את נפשו (שלומין). “…his soul ([in] payment).” Zilber ed.: ואליו הוא נושא את נפשו. Moriah: …אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ שִׁלּוּמִים. “his soul [in] payment.” Me’ah She’arim: אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ. Makor ha-Yirah: M. Avot



## Paragraph 268

###### Sefer HaYirah 268

אל תשב בין העומדים ואל תעמוד בין היושבים. אל תישן בין הערים ואל תהי ער בין הישנים. ולא שמח בין העצבים ולא עצב בין השמחים. כללו של דבר אל ישנה אדם מן המנהג. אך הכל יהיה לטובה וביראת שמים. שאם רואה לצים אל יתלוצץ כמותם.


**Do not**⁵⁴¹ sit among the standing nor stand among the sitting; do not sleep among the waking nor be awake among the sleeping. Do not rejoice among the sorrowful nor be sorrowful among those who rejoice. The rule of the matter: a person should not differ from the custom; however, all will be for good and fear of heaven⁵⁴² if one should see scoffers, he shall not join in their scoffery.⁵⁴³ ch. 10 (?); BT *Ketubot* 17a

## Paragraph 272

###### Sefer HaYirah 272

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/272](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/272)


אל תפרוש מן הצבור כי לא תקבל שכר עמהם. ואם תפרוש ב' מלאכים המלוים לו לאדם מניחין ידיהם אל ראשו ואומרים פלוני שפירש מן הצבור אל יראה בנחמת צבור. אם מנוך צבור פרנס אל תתגאה עליהם.


Do not separate yourself from the community, for you shall not receive a reward with them.⁵⁴⁸ And if you separate, the two accompanying angels that every person has set their hands upon his head and say, “So-and-so, who separated from the community, shall not see the comfort of that community.”⁵⁴⁹, rabbinical ordination, setting that person apart in the realm above as he separated himself in the world below.
Makor ha-Yirah: BT *Ta’anit* 11a; Shulchan Aruch, Orach Chaim § 574:5. If the community has elected you leader, do not lord it over them.⁵⁵⁰

footnotes:
⁵⁴⁸ Zilber ed. המעם, probably a typographical error; Others, עמהם, “with them.”
⁵⁴⁹ Second person/third person confusion in original. “The comfort of that community,” i.e., its reward for its righteousness. The angels placing their hands upon the head of the transgressor is a strange, inverted version of s’michah
⁵⁵⁰ Zilber ed. omits this clause, but the next halachah seems to depend on it.

## Paragraph 275

###### Sefer HaYirah 275

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/275](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/275)


אם אתה שכיר ללמד תורה הזהר בשעת מלאכתך מלעשות מלאכה אחרת אף לא שום דבר כי ברגע יטעו תלמידיך ולא תשמע ומלאכתך מלאכת רמיה. ורמיה בארור. ואף לא לענות ולדבר לשום אדם. ולמוד מאבא חלקיה ז"ל.


And if you are hired to teach Torah take care when you are at your work not to do any other work, not even any other thing, for the moment your students shall err but you shall not hear, your work [is] a work of cheating, and cursed chearting.⁵⁵⁴ Not even to answer or speak to anyone, and learn from Abba Hilkiyah, of blessed memory (BT *Ta’anit* 23a).⁵⁵⁵ 8b. [He also mentions the Ta’anit 23a cite]. Abba Hilkiyah is a grandson of the famous Talmudic figure Choni the Circle-drawer, who like his grandfather saves teh community from drought by praying for rain to fall. In the aggadah cited in the Talmud, he displays exemplary humility and piety.

כלל גדול בתורה דעלך סני לחברך לא תעבד.


The greatest rule in the Torah: That which is hateful to you, do not do to your neighbor.⁵⁶⁸ 31a.

footnotes:
⁵⁶⁸ Quoted in Aramaic. Makor ha-Yirah: BT Shabbat



## Paragraph 284

###### Sefer HaYirah 284

**Now at every**⁵⁶⁹ time and every hour it is important how you shall do the commandments and how you shall keep from transgression in order that you should finish the entire day with goodness.⁵⁷⁰

footnotes:
⁵⁶⁹ Boldface in Moriah, Me’ah She’arim.

The end of the matter when all is said and done:⁵⁷¹ remove from your heart⁵⁷² all thoughts of iniquity;⁵⁷³ only meditation⁵⁷⁴ on Torah and fear of Heaven.

ובמקום מטונף בבית הכסא יחשוב חשבונות. וזה הדבר יסיר מלבו מחשבת טהרה במקום טנופות. ואחרי טהרתו ישוב למחשבתו הראשונה. אך יהיה זהיר בעבודת הבורא. וזה נקרא ערום ביראה.


And instead⁵⁷⁵ of being soiled in the bathroom let him⁵⁷⁶ think of punishment and reward⁵⁷⁷ for this is the thing that will remove from his heart thoughts of pollution and replace them with thoughts of purity. And after his purity⁵⁷⁸ blessing. let him return to his prior thoughts, how⁵⁷⁹ he should be strict in the service of the Creator,⁵⁸⁰ this [one] is called deliberate in the fear [of Heaven].⁵⁸¹

Now, if you are able, you shall not eat before the evening prayer, neither on days of heat nor on days of rain,⁵⁸⁵,” “nine months of winter and three months of hell.”) as our sages, may their memory be for a blessing,⁵⁸⁶ expounded [in BT *Berachot* 10b,⁵⁸⁷ from the verse] “and me, you have cast behind your back,” (1 Kings 14:9) for after you become haughty shall you accept the Kingdom of Heaven?⁵⁸⁸ If you do not eat for the rest of the day the measure increases threefold.⁵⁸⁹

## Paragraph 290

###### Sefer HaYirah 290

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/290](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/290)


בא מבית התפלה והגיע זמן השכיבה יקרא ק"ש כולו. שיש בו רמ"ח תיבות להרחיק כל מזיק מרמ"ח אברים שבו. ויאמר ויהי נועם והשכיבנו בלא חתימה ויפקיד רוחו ביד בוראו. ויתודה וימחול לכל המצערים אותו דארז"ל (מגילה כח) שרי ליה לכל מאן דצערן.


If he⁵⁹⁰ has come from the house of prayer and the time for bed has come, let him recite all of the Sh’ma since there are two hundred forty-eight words in it⁵⁹¹ to keep all danger away from the two hundred forty-eight limbs in him. And let him say the *vihi no’am*,⁵⁹² and the *hashkivenu*⁵⁹³ without a chatimah.⁵⁹⁴ And let him recite verses of Torah, one great and one small.⁵⁹⁵ Then let him commend his soul⁵⁹⁶ to the hand of his Creator. Then let him confess and forgive all who hurt him, according to the sages of our sages (may their memory be for a blessing):⁵⁹⁷ let him forgive for all the hurts they may do him. (BT, Megillah 28a).⁵⁹⁸

footnotes:
⁵⁹⁰ The switch to third person is in the original.
⁵⁹¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: יקרא ק"ש כלו שיש בו רמ"ח תיבות ; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: יקרא קריאת-שמע כֻּלָהּ שיש בָּהּ מאתים וארבעים ושמונה תֵבוֹת. (Resolving the abbreviations and putting the term in the feminine).
⁵⁹² A quotation from Ps. 90 which is part of the traditional bedtime Sh’ma ritual.
⁵⁹³ A prayer asking for God’s protection while sleeping, also part of the traditional bedtime Sh’ma ritual.
⁵⁹⁴ lit., a “seal,” that is, the final line of the blessing which begins, “...ברוך אתה יי”.
⁵⁹⁵ There are multiple verses of Torah in the traditional bedtime Sh’ma ritual; perhaps at this time it was customary to choose verses. At a later time the verses may have become standardized.
⁵⁹⁶ ויפקיד רוחו ביד בוראו echoes בידו אפקיד רוחי, “into His hand I commend my soul,” from Adon Olam.
⁵⁹⁷ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כדארז"ל. Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to: כְדְאָמְרֵי רבותינו, deleting ז"ל (=זכרונם לברכה)
⁵⁹⁸ ּשרי ליה לכל מאן דמצערן. Vilna: שרי ליה (ומחול) לכל מאן דצערן (ליה), perhaps inserting Hebrew translation into the Aramaic and adding an extra pronoun.

Then let him lie down and take off his undershirt in his bed⁶⁰¹ without the beams of his house seeing him naked.⁶⁰² And if he has holy writings there in the place where he sleeps let him cover them.⁶⁰³ And [as for] a chumash⁶⁰⁴ he must set it down in a chest and it must be covered.

300.⁶¹⁵ Now, let him intend when doing the deed⁶¹⁶ [that it be] for the sake of a mitzvah: that he should have children who will walk in the ways of the Lord; let him not intend to enjoy it.⁶¹⁷ 13a. And to continue with the language of our Rabbi, it appears according to the opinion of the Kabbalists, when having sex (תשמיש) he should be careful to avoid a wanton emission of seed. It does not appear here that Zilber is talking about mainstream halakhah.

**Why**,⁶¹⁹ let a man be guided by this entire order as long as he breathes and let him add like this and like that⁶²⁰ to do good and increase the fear of Heaven.⁶²¹

וישלים פרשיותיו עם הצבור בכל שבוע שנים מקרא ואחד תרגום. ואם אין לו תרגום יקרא שנים מקרא ואחד לעז. וטוב יותר מלאמר ג"פ המקרא. כי התרגום היה להבין המקרא למי שאינו בקי כ"ש לעז פירוש שהוא לשון הלעזות.


Let him complete his weekly Torah portions with the community,⁶²² twice in the original and once in [Aramaic] translation.⁶²³ But if he does not have an Aramaic translation he may read twice in the original and once in the vernacular⁶²⁴ which is even better than reading three times⁶²⁵ in the original for the Aramaic translation was for one who was not well-versed; how much the more so is the vernacular commentary for those who speak the vernacular.⁶²⁶ 8a; SA OC § 285. “And if he has no targum…” see :2, :42(?)

ובערב שבת יטרח לסעודת שבת. ואפילו יש לו כמה עבדים יטרח הוא בעצמו לכבוד שבת הנקרא כלה ומלכה. ושקולה כנגד כל המצות. וישתדל בתקוני שבת. כי הא דרבא מלח שבוטא.


**And on the eve**⁶²⁸ of the Sabbath,⁶²⁹ he should prepare for the Sabbath meal.⁶³⁰ And even if he has several servants, he should prepare it by himself⁶³¹ for the honor of the Sabbath, which is called “bride” and “queen” and its value is equal to all the other *mitzvot* on the scale. And let him strive [to complete] Sabbath preparations for this is the thing [Raba] salted a *shibbuta* himself (for the Sabbath). (BT *Shabbat* 119a).⁶³² was probably a mullet. Citation appears in Moriah and in Zilber comment, but there, he has Shabbat 109, without a folio.

⁶²⁸ Boldface in Moriah, Me’ah She’arim.
⁶²⁹ Vilna, וב"ש; others: ובערב שבת.
⁶³⁰ לִסְעוּדַת-שבת.
⁶³¹ The halakhah is about making one’s sabbath preparation on one’s own, as opposed to delegating the tasks.
⁶³² Translation following Jastrow, and according to him a Shibbuta



## Paragraph 307

###### Sefer HaYirah 307

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/307](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/307)


ולפי כחו יקנה מעדנים לעונג שבת.


And as much as he can, let him buy sweets for the joy of the Sabbath.⁶³³

footnotes:
⁶³³ עֹנֶג שָׁבַּת. As in, the Oneg Shabbat.



## Paragraph 308

###### Sefer HaYirah 308

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/308](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/308)


ולא יאכל בע"ש מן המנחה ולמעלה פן יפסיד סעודת שבת. וסמוך לערב יערוך שולחן וכסא ומנורה. ויכין הכל כוסות וקערות.


Now, he should not eat on Sabbath eve⁶³⁴ from Minchah onwards, in case he should spoil⁶³⁵ the Sabbath evening meal. And right before evening he should set the table, chair⁶³⁶ and candelabra and put all of the cups and dishes in their proper place.⁶³⁷ 38b; SA OC § 249:2 “And close to erev…” Gemara, Id.; SA OC § 262.

footnotes:
⁶³⁴ Vilna, Zilber ed.: בע"ש; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: בְּעֶרֶב-שָׁבַּת.
⁶³⁵ פן יפסיד
⁶³⁶ Vilna, Zilber ed.: וכסא; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: כִּסֵּא
⁶³⁷ Makor ha-Yirah: BT Gittin



## Paragraph 309

###### Sefer HaYirah 309

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/309](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/309)


ויהיה זהיר ליין לקדוש היום.


And let him be strict with the wine for the Sanctification of the Day.⁶³⁸. Makor ha-Yirah: BT *Pesachim* 106b; SA OC § 271.

footnotes:
⁶³⁸ קידוש היום. The special Sabbath evening Kiddush



## Paragraph 310

###### Sefer HaYirah 310

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/310](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/310)


ויטול צפרניו בכל ע"ש וישחיז סכינו כמו שדרשו והיה ביום הששי והכינו את זה השחזת סכין. ועוד דרשו. וידעת כי שלום אהלך זה השחזת סכין כי אם קהה הברזל ולא יוכל לחתוך אין זה שלום בית.


He should wash his fingernails⁶³⁹ every Sabbath eve.⁶⁴⁰ And he should sharpen his knife, as our sages⁶⁴¹ expounded: “‘on the sixth day when they shall prepare…’⁶⁴² this [means] sharpening the knife.” (Sifrei to Exod. 16:5). They further expounded: “‘You shall know that your tent is at peace,’⁶⁴³ this [means] the sharpening of the knife,” (Sifrei to Job 5:24) for if the knife is dull and will not cut this is not [fulfilling the commandment of] peace of the home.⁶⁴⁴

footnotes:
⁶³⁹ ויטול צפרניו i.e., a ritual handwashing.
⁶⁴⁰ As above, Vilna, Zilber ed. abbreviate; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve.
⁶⁴¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כמו שדרשו, “as they expounded…” Moriah, Me’ah She’arim insert רַבּוֹתֵינוּ, “as our sages expounded…”
⁶⁴² והיה ביום הששי והכינו את. Vilna, Zilber ed. place square brackets around the direct object marker thus: [את].
⁶⁴³ Translation following Koren.
⁶⁴⁴ Makor ha-Yirah basically mentions the Sifrei quote. This is about doing things regularly, so that they become second nature.



## Paragraph 311

###### Sefer HaYirah 311

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/311](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/311)


וירחץ פניו ידיו ורגליו בחמין לערב אחר שעשה כל צרכיו.


Then he should wash his face, his hands, and his feet⁶⁴⁵ with hot [water] for the evening after he has done all his needs.

footnotes:
⁶⁴⁵ Such as Abraham did in the Torah.



## Paragraph 312

###### Sefer HaYirah 312

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/312](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/312)


וכל מה שיעשה יחשוב לעשות לכבוד שבת.


Everything that he may do, let him think to do [it] for the honor of the Sabbath.



## Paragraph 313

###### Sefer HaYirah 313

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/313](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/313)


ויוסיף מחול על הקדש.


Then let him add from the profane up to the holy.



## Paragraph 314

###### Sefer HaYirah 314

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/314](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/314)


ועם חשכה ימשמש בבגדיו. וידליק בעוד השמש בראש האילנות.


With⁶⁴⁶ [onset of] darkness let him examine⁶⁴⁷ his clothes and let him light [candles] while the sun is still upon the top of the trees.⁶⁴⁸ 35b.

footnotes:
⁶⁴⁶ Vilna, Zilber ed., ואם, “if”; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim, וְעִם , “with” (perhaps a textual emendation.) Translation follows pointed eds.
⁶⁴⁷ יְמַשְׁמֵשׁ , or, “feel,” “touch,” “handle,” “manipulate,” “search.” Perhaps to make sure they are not soiled or that there are no coins or anything in the pockets such that carrying them would violate the Sabbath.
⁶⁴⁸ Technically, one lights Shabbat candles eighteen minutes prior to sundown. R. Yonah says a man should light the candles. He does not assume his audience consists of married men.Makor ha-Yirah: BT Shabbat



## Paragraph 315

###### Sefer HaYirah 315

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/315](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/315)


קדש היום יתפלל של שבת וינוח ויתענג ויסיר מלבו כל אנחה וכל מחשבה. כאלו כל מלאכתו עשויה.


**He sanctifies**⁶⁴⁹ the day⁶⁵⁰. Tense in original shifts to perfect: קדש היום. and he should pray [the order of prayers of] the Sabbath; then he should rest and enjoy himself. And he should banish from his heart all groaning and all thoughts, as if all his work were done.⁶⁵¹.

footnotes:
⁶⁴⁹ Boldface in Moriah.
⁶⁵⁰ i.e., he recites the Sabbath evening kiddush
⁶⁵¹ Zilber’s comment refers to the Mechilta



## Paragraph 316

###### Sefer HaYirah 316

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/316](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/316)


ויכבד שבת בכסות נקיה [ושלחן ערוך] כפי מה שיוכל.


Now let him honor the Sabbath with clean clothing and a set table⁶⁵² as best he can.

footnotes:
⁶⁵² Vilna: ויכבד שבת בכסות נקיה [ושולחן ערוך] כפי מה שיוכל. Zilber ed. omits the bracketed phrase; Moriah and Me’ah She’arim include it without brackets.



## Paragraph 317

###### Sefer HaYirah 317

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/317](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/317)


ויאכל שלש סעודות.


And he should eat three meals.⁶⁵³ (third meal) Saturday afternoon before sundown.

footnotes:
⁶⁵³ Three meals during the course of the Sabbath: Friday evening dinner, Saturday afternoon lunch, and Se’udah Shlisheet



## Paragraph 318

###### Sefer HaYirah 318

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/318](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/318)


אל ידבר שום דבר בחפציו הצריכים לו. אך בחפצי שמים.


Let him not speak a word of his desires that he needs,⁶⁵⁴ but only the desires of Heaven.

footnotes:
⁶⁵⁴ אל ידבר שום דבר בחפציו הצריכים לו. Let him not mention his own petty personal needs.



## Paragraph 319

###### Sefer HaYirah 319

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/319](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/319)


חשבונות שעברו מותר.


Prior reckonings are permitted.⁶⁵⁵

footnotes:
⁶⁵⁵ חשבונות שעברו מותר. I.e., to be spoken of during Shabbat, but not current accounting.Makor ha-Yirah: SA __ § 307:6 and see there for what is called “prior reckonings.”



## Paragraph 320

###### Sefer HaYirah 320

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/320](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/320)


ואל ירבה שיחה אם לא בדברי תורה ויראת שמים כי אף דברי תורה התירו בקושי.


Let him not increase conversation if it is not words of Torah and fear of Heaven, for even peaceful requests permit difficulty.⁶⁵⁶

footnotes:
⁶⁵⁶ Vilna, Zilber ed.: כי אף (ד"ת) (=דברי תורה) [שאלת שלום] התירו בקושי. Moriah: כי אף דברי תורה (שאלת שלום) הִתִּירוּ בְקֹשִׁי. Me’ah She’arim: כי אף שאלת שלום התירו בקושי. Translation following Me’ah She’arim, Probable textual emendation.



## Paragraph 321

###### Sefer HaYirah 321

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/321](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/321)


ואל יפסיע פסיעה גסה אם לא ללכת לדבר מצוה. ולא שום טורח אל יעשה.


Let him not march with hasty steps⁶⁵⁷ 10b if not to go to do a mitzvah, and no manner of preparation let him not do.⁶⁵⁸ 6b.

footnotes:
⁶⁵⁷ Translation following Jastrow, BT Ta’anit
⁶⁵⁸ Double negative in the original. Makor ha-Yirah: BT Berachot



## Paragraph 322

###### Sefer HaYirah 322

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/322](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/322)


ואל ידבר מאוהביו שמתו ומכל עגמת נפש. כללו של דבר נקרא יום עונג ומנוחה.


Let him not speak of loved ones that died, or any manner of grief of the soul. The rule of the matter: the Sabbath is called a day of pleasure and rest.



## Paragraph 323

###### Sefer HaYirah 323

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/323](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/323)


ויוסיף מן החול על הקדש. ואל יוציא דמעה מעיניו. וישמרהו כהלכותיו ע"פ רבותינו ע"ה. במוצ"ש יבדיל בתפלה ועל הכוס.


323a.⁶⁵⁹ Now let him add from the profane upon the holy.⁶⁶⁰ 33a; SA §§ 294, 296. and let no tear come forth from his eyes. But let him keep it according to the laws taught by our Sages, may peace be upon them.⁶⁶¹
b. **On Saturday evening**⁶⁶² let him make a separation with prayer and over the cup.⁶⁶³, the ritual separation of the sacred time of Shabbat from the profane time of the rest of the week, which includes a candle with multiple wicks, aromatic spices, and a cup of wine. 
Makor ha-Yirah: BT *Berachot* 33a.

footnotes:
⁶⁵⁹ Zilber ed. accidentally numbers #323 twice.
⁶⁶⁰ Makor ha-Yirah: BT Berachot
⁶⁶¹ Vilna, Zilber ed.: וישמרהו כהלכותיו ע"פ רבותינו ע"ה. Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: …על פי רבותינו עליהם השלום.
⁶⁶² Vilna, Zilber ed.: במוצ"ש; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: .במוצָאֵי שבת Lit., “On the going-out of Shabbat,” i.e., Saturday after nightfall, already יום ראשון, the first day of the Jewish week. Boldface in Moriah.
⁶⁶³ I.e., make havdalah



## Paragraph 324

###### Sefer HaYirah 324

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/324](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/324)


לא הבדיל בתפלה ובא לעשות צרכיו (מלאכה האסורה בשבת) קודם שיבדיל. יברך בא"י אמ"ה המבדיל כו' ויעשה צרכיו.


If he did not separate with prayer and he must attend to his needs⁶⁶⁴ prior to separating, let him bless [as follows]: “You are blessed, ETERNAL our God, ruler of the universe, who separates between sacred and profane,”⁶⁶⁵ then let him take care of his needs. ⁶⁶⁶ in the name of *Rosh* (Asheri) that he needs to say it with the Name and Kingship” I.e., not just בא"י but also אמ"ה.

footnotes:
⁶⁶⁴ .ובא לעשות צרכיו Perhaps, “it comes that he must take care of his needs…”
⁶⁶⁵ Vilna, Zilber ed. abbreviate the blessing: בא"י המבדיל וכו’ ויעשה. Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve it in full.
⁶⁶⁶ Makor ha-Yirah: “The Tur



## Paragraph 325

###### Sefer HaYirah 325

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/325](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/325)


ויסדר שלחנו במוצ"ש אע"פ שאין צריך אלא לכזית. וילוה המלך בשירות ותשבחות. ובע"ש ויו"ט ינהג בעצמו כמו שאמרנו. כללו של דבר לכל זמן. בעת שמחה בפורים ירבה בשמחה ובמשתה. אך שיהיה לבו לשמים שלא יצא מפיו דבר ערוה וניבול פה. בעת עצב עצב.


Then he should set his table on Saturday night, even though he only needs to eat as much as an olive.⁶⁶⁷ Then he should send out the King with song and praise.⁶⁶⁸ **And on festival eve**,⁶⁶⁹ let him conduct himself as we have said on Sabbath eve. The rule of the matter: for every time at a season of rejoicing, rejoice. At Purim let him increase joy and drinking. But his heart should be [directed] to Heaven so that no word of immorality or obscenity comes out⁶⁷⁰ of his mouth. And in a season of sorrow, be sorrowful.

footnotes:
⁶⁶⁷ A halachic standard for the smallest amount of bread one must eat in order to say the Birkat ha’Mazon.
⁶⁶⁸ Vilna, Zilber ed., Moriah: המלך. Me’ah She’arim: המלכה, “the queen,” probably a textual emendation, but this is the usual personification of Shabbat. To personify Shabbat as a King is more unusual.Zilber: BT Shabbat 119b.
⁶⁶⁹ Boldface in Moriah, Me’ah She’arim, but Zilber ed. does not even assign a new line number. Vilna, Zilber ed.: .ובע"ש וי"ט ינהג כמו שאמרנו “And on Sabbath eve and festival eve let him conduct himself as we have said.” Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: ובערב יום טוב יִנהַג כמוֹ שאמרנו בערב-שבת. “And on festival eve let him conduct himself as we said about Sabbath eve.”
⁶⁷⁰ Zilber ed.: יוציא. Others: יֵצֵא



## Paragraph 326

###### Sefer HaYirah 326

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/326](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/326)


משנכנס אב ממעטין בשמחה.


When [the Hebrew month of] Av enters, joy decreases.⁶⁷¹ 26:2. SA OC § 551.

footnotes:
⁶⁷¹ Due to the mournful nature of the fast of the 9th of Av. Vilna, Zilber ed.: ממעטין; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: מְמַעֲטִים.Makor ha-Yirah: M. Ta’anit



## Paragraph 327

###### Sefer HaYirah 327

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/327](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/327)


משנכנס אלול עד מוצאי יוה"כ יהא חרד וירא מאימת הדין.


When [the Hebrew month of] Elul enters, until the night after the Day of Atonement, be trembling and fearful from [being] scared of the Judgment.⁶⁷²

footnotes:
⁶⁷² יהא חרד וירא מאימת הדין. Makor ha-Yirah: Pirkei d’Rabbi Eliezer; SA (OC?) § 581.



## Paragraph 328

###### Sefer HaYirah 328

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/328](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/328)


תמיד בכל חדש יום א' או יותר ישב בתענית. או לכל הפחות יאכל לחם ומים ואותו יום יהיה בבכי ובמספד. בכי. זה הבוכה בדמעות. ומספד. כאדם הסופד על מתו. מספר מעשיו ואמר אוי לי שעשיתי חטא פלוני. אוי לי כמה חייבתי נפשי למלכו של עולם. וזו היא התשובה המעולה שנאמר' ע"י יואל הנביא (קרעו לבבכם וכו') [וגם עתה כו'].


In every month, one should always sit one or more days⁶⁷³ in fasting or at least eating [only] bread and water. Now on that day⁶⁷⁴ he should sit in crying and lamentation. Crying: this is the one who cries with tears. Lamentation: like a man who mourns his death, recounting his deeds and saying “Woe to me! That I have done such-and-such sin!⁶⁷⁵ Woe to me, that I am liable for the death-penalty⁶⁷⁶ unto the Eternal King!” But this is⁶⁷⁷ the exalted repentance said by⁶⁷⁸ Joel the prophet: “Now tear your hearts, not your clothes and turn back to the Lord,” (Joel 2:13)⁶⁷⁹ with fasting, with crying, and with lamenting.⁶⁸⁰

footnotes:
⁶⁷³ Vilna, Zilber ed.: יום א; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: יום אחד.
⁶⁷⁴ Moriah, Zilber ed.: ואותו-היו; Vilna, Me’ah She’arim: ואותו יום.
⁶⁷⁵ אוי לי שעשיתי חטא פלוני. Me’ah She’arim puts the phrase שעשיתי חטא פלוני in smaller, unvocalized font.
⁶⁷⁶ Lit., “how I have obligated my soul...”
⁶⁷⁷ Vilna, Zilber ed.: וזו היא; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: וזוהי
⁶⁷⁸ Vilna, Zilber ed.: ע"י; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to על ידי.
⁶⁷⁹ Vilna [וגם עתה וגו’]  (וקרעו לבבכם ואל בגדיכם וגו) . Vilna also puts a reference to Joel 2:12 in square brackets: “Even now, swears the ETERNAL, turn back to me with all your hearts…” Moriah continues the quotation from Joel 2:13 through “…and return to the Lord,” while others truncate at “...rend your clothes.”
⁶⁸⁰ “with fasting…with lamenting.” appears in Moriah only.



## Paragraph 329

###### Sefer HaYirah 329

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/329](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/329)


ויתהא על עוונות נעוריו שעשה ואל ישוב לעשות עוד.


Let him gaze⁶⁸¹ upon the sins of his youth that he has done and never return to them any more.

footnotes:
⁶⁸¹ וְיִתְהֶה, but Moriah וְיִתְהֶא (qal: impf: 3ms: תהה or תהא) Or “regret” but על afterwards appears to be “upon.”



## Paragraph 330

###### Sefer HaYirah 330

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/330](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/330)


ובכל יום יתוודה על עונותיו הגלוים לו בהויתן. ויהיה מודה ועוזב. ולא טובל ושרץ בידו.


And every day he confesses⁶⁸² over his sins that are known⁶⁸³ to him as they stand,⁶⁸⁴ so he should be gratefully acknowledging⁶⁸⁵ and letting go [of them] and not [like] one who enters a ritual bath with a lizard in his hand.⁶⁸⁶

footnotes:
⁶⁸² Vilna, Zilber ed.: מתודה; Moriah: יִתְוַדֶה; Me’ah She’arim lacks the phrase (see below).
⁶⁸³ עונותיו הגלוים, more lit., “his sins that are revealed to him.” Me’ah She’arim omits the entire phrase: .על עונות נעוריו שעשה ואל ישוב לעשות עוד ובכול יום יתודה על... Apparently a scrivener’s error -- haplography -- caused by the appearance of the phrase על עונות twice in two lines.
⁶⁸⁴ Vilna בהויתן; Zilber ed. כהויתן, Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: כַּהֲיָתָן. Translation follows Zilber ed. and Jastrow, ref to JT, Ber. II 4d.
⁶⁸⁵ מודה
⁶⁸⁶ Just as one cannot become ritually pure while holding an impure object such as a lizard; so too one cannot fully repent for past deeds while still holding onto them.



## Paragraph 331

###### Sefer HaYirah 331

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/331](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/331)


ויתפלל בכל יום כפי צחות לשונו על כל חולי בנ"י שיתרפאו. ועל הבריאים שלא יחלו. ושינצלו מכל נזק. ושיציל הקב"ה את בנ"י מכל רו"ר. ומדקדוקי עניות. ומכל מיני פורעניות המתרגשות ובאות לעולם. ושיתיר כל אסירי עמו. ושיתיר כל חבלי היולדות. ועל חשוכי בנים שיתן להם זרע טוב וכשר. ועל אותם שיש להם בנים שיהיו ביראת ה'. ועל כולם שישמור הקב"ה שארית תורתו. וינקום את נקמתם במהרה בימינו. ועל עם הקדש שישובו בתשובה שלימה. ויקבלם ויחזירם בתשובה שלימה לפניו.


**So let him pray**⁶⁸⁷ every day as beautifully as he can⁶⁸⁸ for all of the ill of the holy nation that they may be healed, and for all the healthy ones that they may not fall ill⁶⁸⁹ and that they may be saved from all damage. And that the Holy One, Blessed be He,⁶⁹⁰ may save his people Israel⁶⁹¹ from all enemies⁶⁹² and from evil spirits⁶⁹³ and from the sufferings of poverty and from all kinds of divine punishments which rush to enter the world,⁶⁹⁴ and that He may free all the captives of His people and that He may untie the bonds of the women in the throes of birth⁶⁹⁵ and that He may bring back to the fear of Him all those forcibly seized by the Nations.⁶⁹⁶ And for all those lacking children⁶⁹⁷ 28a. that He may give them good and kosher seed. And for those that have them, that they may live in fear of the Lord. And for all of them, that the Holy One, Blessed be He,⁶⁹⁸ may guard the remnant of His people.⁶⁹⁹ And that He may execute their judgment speedily and in our days. And for the holy people, let them return in complete repentance, that he may receive them and take them back in complete repentance before Him.

footnotes:
⁶⁸⁷ Boldface in Moriah, Me’ah She’arim.
⁶⁸⁸ כפי צחוּת לשונו , lit. “according to the brightness of his language”
⁶⁸⁹ Vilna, Zilber ed. insert ח"ו (=חס ושלום) “God forbid.”
⁶⁹⁰ Vilna, Zilber ed.: הקב"ה; Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to הקדוש ברוך הוא
⁶⁹¹ Translation follows Zilber ed., עמו ישראל; Vilna בנ"י, Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve to בני ישראל “children of Israel” or “Israelites.”
⁶⁹² Vilna, מכל אויב, others, מכל גוים, “from all of the nations,” or “all the gentile enemies.”
⁶⁹³ Me’ah She’arim, מרוחות רעות, others מרוח רעה, “from an evil spirit.”
⁶⁹⁴ Translation following Jastrow and JT Ber. V 8d.
⁶⁹⁵ ושיתיר את כל חבלי היולדות. BT Shabbat 32a, M. Sotah I:5
⁶⁹⁶ Vilna: בידי עמים; Zilber ed. בידי גוים, Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: ביד גוים. That is bring Jewish children taken from their parents and raised as Christians (such as the later case of Edgardo Mortara) and forced converts back to Judaism.
⁶⁹⁷ Translation following Jastrow and BT Ervuin
⁶⁹⁸ As above, Vilna, zilber ed. abbreviate הקב"ה, Moriah, Me’ah She’arim resolve.
⁶⁹⁹ Vilna, Zilber ed. שישמור שארית תורתו, “that He may guard the remnant of his Torah.” Moriah, Me’ah She’arim: שישמור שארית (תורתו) [עמו]. The emended version makes more sense.



## Paragraph 332

###### Sefer HaYirah 332

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/332](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sefer%20HaYirah/r/332)


ויקרא בכל יום בפ' ועתה ישראל וגו' שכולה יראת שמים למען יזכור תמיד הבורא ולא יחטא:


Every day one should read the Torah portion beginning “And now Israel, what does the Lord require of you?” (Deut. 10:12).⁷⁰⁰ For all of it⁷⁰¹ is fear of Heaven, so that he may always remember the Creator and that he may not sin.

footnotes:
⁷⁰⁰ Me’ah She’arim truncates the quotation at ועתה ישראל, “and now, Israel…”; others quote through “…what does the Lord your God require of you?” (The next part of the verse is “…but to fear the Lord your God…” which explains how the prooftext works.)
⁷⁰¹ Zilber ed.: שכולו; others, שכולהּ.
